[![How to Jailbreak an IPod Touch 4th Generation](https://content.instructables.com/F7X/PC1Q/HVGXLKX8/F7XPC1QHVGXLKX8.jpg?frame=true&width=933&height=1024&fit=bounds)](https://content.instructables.com/F7X/PC1Q/HVGXLKX8/F7XPC1QHVGXLKX8.jpg)

PLEASE READ!

As you may know, jailbreaking an iPod is breaching apples security on the divide, therefor breaching any warrant. you should read up on jailbreaking before beginning this project.

please do not report me for doing this tutorial, it is your choice to make if you want to jailbreak your iDevice.

**I DO NOT** **RECOMMEND** **JAILBREAKING YOUR IPHONE!**

This tutorial explains how to jailbreak an iPod running IOS 6.1.x(0,1, 2 3,4,6)

ps. this is the untethered jailbreak.

note: I did not come up with this way on my own, I used [http://www.iphonehacks.com/2013/03/jailbreak-ios-6...](http://www.iphonehacks.com/2013/03/jailbreak-ios-6-1-3-using-redsn0w.html)

and sites describing posixspwn.

enjoy!

## Step 1: The Materials and the Downloads

![The Materials and the Downloads](https://content.instructables.com/FFD/2BTE/HVGXLLM5/FFD2BTEHVGXLLM5.jpg?width=270&auto=webp)

[![The Materials and the Downloads](https://content.instructables.com/FFD/2BTE/HVGXLLM5/FFD2BTEHVGXLLM5.jpg?frame=true&width=800&height=1024&fit=bounds&crop=3:2)](https://content.instructables.com/FFD/2BTE/HVGXLLM5/FFD2BTEHVGXLLM5.jpg)[![The Materials and the Downloads](https://content.instructables.com/FMW/2QXZ/HVGXLL1N/FMW2QXZHVGXLL1N.jpg?frame=true&width=400&height=1024&fit=bounds&crop=3:2)](https://content.instructables.com/FMW/2QXZ/HVGXLL1N/FMW2QXZHVGXLL1N.jpg)

The first thing you need is to make sure you have the right stuff.

You will need...

\-An iPod touch 4th generation running IOS 6.1.(0,1,2,3)

\-A computer to tether it to.(i used my macbook air)

a charging USB cord to connect the iPod to the computer(standard apple cord)

The ability to download software

BEFORE YOU JAILBREAK:

1-Read up on it. Make sure you know what your doing.

2-Back up your iDevice through iTunes or iCloud.

3-Make sure you are running the latest version of iTunes.

4-If your device has a passcode lock, turn it off! you can put it back on when the jailbreaking is finished(after the end of this tutorial)

NOW TO THE DOWNLOADING

You will need to download two separate things, the application redsn0w and the firmware.

step 1-download redsn0w. Go to [http://www.iphonehacks.com/download-redsn0w](http://www.iphonehacks.com/download-redsn0w) (again, i did not find this link on my own) and click on version **Redsn0w 0.9.15 beta 3.** click on your computer type(windows, mac) and you will be brought to the download page. Download the file and wait.

while you wait, you can download the firmware. Go to [http://www.redsn0w.us](http://www.redsn0w.us/) . Once on the site, go to the **download IOS-jailbreak** tab at the top left corner.

Then scroll down to the **get IOS.com** widget.

**NOW PAY ATTENTION**

Ther are three categories you fill in. The iDevice, the model and the IOS version. fill in the first slot with whatever device you have. Fill the next slot with your iPod model(or iPad, or iPhone). The last slot is the one you won't expect. YOU MUST PUT IN IOS **6.0.0** NO MATTER WHAT IOS VERSION YOU ACTULY HAVE. When this is done, click the download button and wait.

## Step 2: The Jailbreak

![The Jailbreak](https://content.instructables.com/F36/270K/HVGXLF1F/F36270KHVGXLF1F.png?width=270&auto=webp) ![The Jailbreak](https://content.instructables.com/FLZ/RNR2/HVGXLLKA/FLZRNR2HVGXLLKA.jpg?width=270&auto=webp)

[![The Jailbreak](https://content.instructables.com/F36/270K/HVGXLF1F/F36270KHVGXLF1F.png?frame=true&width=900&height=1024&fit=bounds&crop=3:2)](https://content.instructables.com/F36/270K/HVGXLF1F/F36270KHVGXLF1F.png)[![The Jailbreak](https://content.instructables.com/FLZ/RNR2/HVGXLLKA/FLZRNR2HVGXLLKA.jpg?frame=true&width=300&height=1024&fit=bounds&crop=3:2)](https://content.instructables.com/FLZ/RNR2/HVGXLLKA/FLZRNR2HVGXLLKA.jpg)Once redsn0w and the firmware are downloaded, drag both to the desktop for easy access. Open the redsn0w folder and you will see five files and applications. Open the application redsn0w. Now plug your i device into your computer using your cable.

DO NOT CLICK JAILBREAK YET!

First click on **extras.** then go to **select IPSW.** IPSW is iPhone software. navigate to **iPod4,1\_6.0\_10A403\_Restore.ipsw** (this is the firmware you just downloaded). it looks like a white lego block with a music note on one side and an iPod on the other. Click on it and a message will pop up. Click **OK** and go back to the main page and click **jailbreak**. Now since I already jailbroke my iPod I can't show you picture of a few steps, so I will do my best to describe them. You will need to select jailbreak options. MAKE SURE CYDIA IS SELECTED! you will eventually be told to put your divice into DFU mode.(device firmware upgrade) To do this follow the instructions

1-Hold the Power button on your iOS device down for 3 seconds:

2- Now simultaneously hold the Home button and keep the two buttons pressed for 10 seconds:

3- Now release the Power button while keeping the Home button pressed until Redsn0w detects the device:

your iDevice should reboot now

REMEMBER TO RELEASE THE HOME BUTTON

you will be notified when the process is complete. click OK then QUIT

**WARNING: JAILBREAKING WILL TAKE SOME TIME. JUST WAIT AND BE PATIENT.**

while you are waiting, read up on teethed and unteathed jailbreaking.

## Step 3: Now the Device...

![Now the Device...](https://content.instructables.com/FM5/1FBG/HVGXLKU2/FM51FBGHVGXLKU2.png?width=270&auto=webp)

[![Now the Device...](https://content.instructables.com/FM5/1FBG/HVGXLKU2/FM51FBGHVGXLKU2.png?frame=true&width=400&height=1024&fit=bounds)](https://content.instructables.com/FM5/1FBG/HVGXLKU2/FM51FBGHVGXLKU2.png)[![Now the Device...](https://content.instructables.com/F07/TWHS/HVGXLM6F/F07TWHSHVGXLM6F.png?frame=true&width=400&height=1024&fit=bounds)](https://content.instructables.com/F07/TWHS/HVGXLM6F/F07TWHSHVGXLM6F.png)

When the jailbreak is done, your device will reboot again. THIS MAY TAKE UP TO TEN MINNUTS. AGAIN, BE PATIENT!

you will see your home screen, but cydia will not be on it. DO NOT PANIC.

You now need to boot teethed jailbreak. You will switch to unteathed in a bit.

to start off...

launch redsn0w again on your computer and keep your iPod connected. go to **extras**, then to **select IPSW**. **select** **iPod4,1\_6.0\_10A403\_Restore.ipsw** again, but this time go to **just boot.**

Follow the onscreen instructions about turning your iPod off and the DFU.

Your iDevice will reboot again, with a pineapple logo, indicating a teethed boot. WAIT AGAIN.

When your iDevice turns back on, unlock it and look for the cydia logo. It looks like a brown and white box.

If you can't find it, look for it in spotlight.

YOUR NOT DONE YET

## Step 4: The Final Step:Untethering Your Divice

please go to a tutorial on cydia. I recommend [http://www.ipadforums.net/jailbreaking-hacking-tut...](http://www.ipadforums.net/jailbreaking-hacking-tutorials-how-guides/67553-tutorial-cydia-beginners-guide-jailbreak-app-store.html).

Once you know the ropes, go to **search.** search **p0sixspwn.** click install at the top right(once it is installed, that tab will be switched to modify, like mine). your iPod will restart and when it comes back on, your done.

## Step 5: Some Recommendations

Now that your iPod is jailbroken, here are some Cyida apps.

look up how to install them online

1-winterboard - to customize your iPod(note, you will need to download mods through winter board)

2-AppCake- to get paid apps for free(NOTE: NOT LEAGAL)

3-activator - for instant and easy flow on your iPod

Have fun

\-Shadow513

class="_socialHandles_2etee_129"></div></div><div class="_stepsContainer_1vuso_25"><section class="_step_ty83r_1"><div class="_subtitle_20_6jn54_93 _artifakt_element_6jn54_2 _stepBody_1dglo_1"><p>PLEASE READ!</p><p>As you may know, jailbreaking an iPod is breaching apples security on the divide, therefor breaching any warrant. you should read up on jailbreaking before beginning this project.</p><p>please do not report me for doing this tutorial, it is your choice to make if you want to jailbreak your iDevice.</p><p><strong>I DO NOT </strong><strong>RECOMMEND</strong><strong> JAILBREAKING YOUR IPHONE!</strong></p><p>This tutorial explains how to jailbreak an iPod running IOS 6.1.x(0,1, 2 3,4,6)</p><p>ps. this is the untethered jailbreak.</p><p>note: I did not come up with this way on my own, I used <a href="http://www.iphonehacks.com/2013/03/jailbreak-ios-6-1-3-using-redsn0w.html" rel="nofollow noopener noreferrer">http://www.iphonehacks.com/2013/03/jailbreak-ios-6...</a> </p><p>and sites describing posixspwn.</p><p>enjoy!</p></div></section><section class="_step_ty83r_1"><h2 class="_stepTitle_ty83r_26">Step 1: The Materials and the Downloads</h2><div class="_mediaset_1jc2o_1"><div class="_mobilePhotoset_1jc2o_175"><div class="_mobileScrollContainer_1jc2o_196"><div class="_mobileImageWrapper_1jc2o_226"><img class="_mobilePhotosetImage_1jc2o_261" alt="The Materials and the Downloads" loading="eager" src="https://content.instructables.com/FFD/2BTE/HVGXLLM5/FFD2BTEHVGXLLM5.jpg?width=270&amp;auto=webp"></div><div class="_mobileImageWrapper_1jc2o_226"><img class="_mobilePhotosetImage_1jc2o_261" alt="The Materials and the Downloads" loading="lazy" src="https://content.instructables.com/FC2/AAOB/HVGXLLPR/FC2AAOBHVGXLLPR.jpg?width=270&amp;auto=webp"></div><div src="https://content.instructables.com/FMW/2QXZ/HVGXLL1N/FMW2QXZHVGXLL1N.jpg?width=270&amp;auto=webp"></div></div></div><div class="_photoset_1jc2o_12"><div class="_photosetRow_1jc2o_38"><div class="_photosetCell_1jc2o_51"><div class="_photosetImage_1jc2o_55"><a class="_galleryLink_1jc2o_145" href="https://content.instructables.com/FFD/2BTE/HVGXLLM5/FFD2BTEHVGXLLM5.jpg" data-download="https://content.instructables.com/FFD/2BTE/HVGXLLM5/FFD2BTEHVGXLLM5.jpg" data-fileid="FFD2BTEHVGXLLM5"><img loading="eager" alt="The Materials and the Downloads" width="792" height="528" src="https://content.instructables.com/FFD/2BTE/HVGXLLM5/FFD2BTEHVGXLLM5.jpg?frame=true&amp;width=800&amp;height=1024&amp;fit=bounds&amp;crop=3:2"></a></div></div><div class="_photosetCol_1jc2o_46"><div class="_photosetCell_1jc2o_51"><div class="_photosetImage_1jc2o_55"><a class="_galleryLink_1jc2o_145" href="https://content.instructables.com/FC2/AAOB/HVGXLLPR/FC2AAOBHVGXLLPR.jpg" data-download="https://content.instructables.com/FC2/AAOB/HVGXLLPR/FC2AAOBHVGXLLPR.jpg" data-fileid="FC2AAOBHVGXLLPR"><img loading="eager" alt="The Materials and the Downloads" width="392" height="261" src="https://content.instructables.com/FC2/AAOB/HVGXLLPR/FC2AAOBHVGXLLPR.jpg?frame=true&amp;width=400&amp;height=1024&amp;fit=bounds&amp;crop=3:2"></a></div></div><div class="_photosetCell_1jc2o_51"><div class="_photosetImage_1jc2o_55"><a class="_galleryLink_1jc2o_145" href="https://content.instructables.com/FMW/2QXZ/HVGXLL1N/FMW2QXZHVGXLL1N.jpg" data-download="https://content.instructables.com/FMW/2QXZ/HVGXLL1N/FMW2QXZHVGXLL1N.jpg" data-fileid="FMW2QXZHVGXLL1N"><img loading="eager" alt="The Materials and the Downloads" width="392" height="261" src="https://content.instructables.com/FMW/2QXZ/HVGXLL1N/FMW2QXZHVGXLL1N.jpg?frame=true&amp;width=400&amp;height=1024&amp;fit=bounds&amp;crop=3:2"></a></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="_subtitle_20_6jn54_93 _artifakt_element_6jn54_2 _stepBody_1dglo_1"><p>The first thing you need is to make sure you have the right stuff.</p><p>You will need...</p><p>-An iPod touch 4th generation running IOS 6.1.(0,1,2,3)</p><p>-A computer to tether it to.(i used my macbook air)</p><p>a charging USB cord to connect the iPod to the computer(standard apple cord)</p><p>The ability to download software</p><p>BEFORE YOU JAILBREAK:</p><p>1-Read up on it. Make sure you know what your doing.</p><p>2-Back up your iDevice through iTunes or iCloud.</p><p>3-Make sure you are running the latest version of iTunes.</p><p>4-If your device has a passcode lock, turn it off! you can put it back on when the jailbreaking is finished(after the end of this tutorial)</p><p>NOW TO THE DOWNLOADING</p><p>You will need to download two separate things, the application redsn0w and the firmware.</p><p>step 1-download redsn0w. Go to <a href="http://www.iphonehacks.com/download-redsn0w" rel="nofollow noopener noreferrer">http://www.iphonehacks.com/download-redsn0w</a> (again, i did not find this link on my own) and click on version <strong>Redsn0w 0.9.15 beta 3. </strong>click on your computer type(windows, mac) and you will be brought to the download page. Download the file and wait. </p><p>while you wait, you can download the firmware. Go to  <a href="http://www.redsn0w.us" rel="nofollow noopener noreferrer">http://www.redsn0w.us </a> . Once on the site, go to the <strong>download IOS-jailbreak </strong>tab at the top left corner.</p><p>Then scroll down to the <strong>get IOS.com </strong>widget.</p><p><strong>NOW PAY ATTENTION</strong></p><p>Ther are three categories you fill in. The iDevice, the model and the IOS version. fill in the first slot with whatever device you have. Fill the next slot with your iPod model(or iPad, or iPhone). The last slot is the one you won't expect.  YOU MUST PUT IN IOS <strong>6.0.0</strong> NO MATTER WHAT IOS VERSION YOU ACTULY HAVE. When this is done, click the download button and wait.</p></div></section><section class="_step_ty83r_1"><h2 class="_stepTitle_ty83r_26">Step 2: The Jailbreak</h2><div class="_mediaset_1jc2o_1"><div class="_mobilePhotoset_1jc2o_175"><div class="_mobileScrollContainer_1jc2o_196"><div class="_mobileImageWrapper_1jc2o_226"><img class="_mobilePhotosetImage_1jc2o_261" alt="The Jailbreak" loading="eager" src="https://content.instructables.com/F36/270K/HVGXLF1F/F36270KHVGXLF1F.png?width=270&amp;auto=webp"></div><div class="_mobileImageWrapper_1jc2o_226"><img href="https://content.instructables.com/FN4/81F3/HVGXLLA4/FN481F3HVGXLLA4.jpg" data-download="https://content.instructables.com/FN4/81F3/HVGXLLA4/FN481F3HVGXLLA4.jpg" class="_photosetCell_1jc2o_51"><div class="_photosetImage_1jc2o_55"><a class="_galleryLink_1jc2o_145" href="https://content.instructables.com/FLZ/RNR2/HVGXLLKA/FLZRNR2HVGXLLKA.jpg" data-download="https://content.instructables.com/FLZ/RNR2/HVGXLLKA/FLZRNR2HVGXLLKA.jpg" data-fileid="FLZRNR2HVGXLLKA"><img loading="eager" alt="The Jailbreak" width="292" height="195" src="https://content.instructables.com/FLZ/RNR2/HVGXLLKA/FLZRNR2HVGXLLKA.jpg?frame=true&amp;width=300&amp;height=1024&amp;fit=bounds&amp;crop=3:2"></a></div></div><div class="_photosetCell_1jc2o_51"><div class="_photosetImage_1jc2o_55"><a class="_galleryLink_1jc2o_145" href="https://content.instructables.com/FBY/OVI3/HVGXLF1K/FBYOVI3HVGXLF1K.png" data-download="https://content.instructables.com/FBY/OVI3/HVGXLF1K/FBYOVI3HVGXLF1K.png" data-fileid="FBYOVI3HVGXLF1K"><img loading="eager" alt="The Jailbreak" width="292" height="195" src="https://content.instructables.com/FBY/OVI3/HVGXLF1K/FBYOVI3HVGXLF1K.png?frame=true&amp;width=300&amp;height=1024&amp;fit=bounds&amp;crop=3:2"></a></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="_subtitle_20_6jn54_93 _artifakt_element_6jn54_2 _stepBody_1dglo_1"><p>Once redsn0w and the firmware are downloaded, drag both to the desktop for easy access. Open the redsn0w folder and you will see five files and applications. Open the application redsn0w. Now plug your i device into your computer using your cable. </p><p>DO NOT CLICK JAILBREAK YET!</p><p>First click on <strong>extras. </strong>then go to <strong>select IPSW. </strong>IPSW is iPhone software. navigate to <strong>iPod4,1_6.0_10A403_Restore.ipsw </strong>(this is the firmware you just downloaded). it looks like a white lego block with a music note on one side and an iPod on the other. Click on it and a message will pop up. Click <strong>OK </strong>and go back to the main page and click <strong>jailbreak</strong>. Now since I already jailbroke my iPod I can't show you picture of a few steps, so I will do my best to describe them. You will need to select jailbreak options. MAKE SURE CYDIA IS SELECTED! you will eventually be told to put your divice into DFU mode.(device firmware upgrade) To do this follow the instructions</p><p>1-Hold the Power button on your iOS device down for 3 seconds:</p><p>2- Now simultaneously hold the Home button and keep the two buttons pressed for 10 seconds: </p><p>3- Now release the Power button while keeping the Home button pressed until Redsn0w detects the device:</p><p>your iDevice should reboot now</p><p>REMEMBER TO RELEASE THE HOME BUTTON</p><p>you will be notified when the process is complete. click OK then QUIT</p><p><strong>WARNING: JAILBREAKING WILL TAKE SOME TIME. JUST WAIT AND BE PATIENT.</strong></p><p>while you are waiting, read up on teethed and unteathed jailbreaking. </p></div></section><section class="_step_ty83r_1"><h2 class="_stepTitle_ty83r_26">Step 3: Now the Device...</h2><div class="_mediaset_1jc2o_1"><div class="_mobilePhotoset_1jc2o_175"><div class="_mobileScrollContainer_1jc2o_196"><div class="_mobileImageWrapper_1jc2o_226"><img class="_subtitle_20_6jn54_93 _artifakt_element_6jn54_2 _stepBody_1dglo_1"><p>When the jailbreak is done, your device will reboot again. THIS MAY TAKE UP TO TEN MINNUTS. AGAIN, BE PATIENT!</p><p>you will see your home screen, but cydia will not be on it. DO NOT PANIC. </p><p>You now need to boot teethed jailbreak. You will switch to unteathed in a bit.</p><p>to start off...</p><p>launch redsn0w again on your computer and keep your iPod connected. go to<strong> extras</strong>, then to <strong>select IPSW</strong>. <strong>select </strong><strong>iPod4,1_6.0_10A403_Restore.ipsw </strong>again, but this time go to <strong>just boot.</strong></p><p><strong><br></strong></p><p>Follow the onscreen instructions about turning your iPod off and the DFU.</p><p>Your iDevice will reboot again, with a pineapple logo, indicating a teethed boot. WAIT AGAIN.</p><p>When your iDevice turns back on, unlock it and look for the cydia logo. It looks like a brown and white box.</p><p>If you can't find it, look for it in spotlight.</p><p>YOUR NOT DONE YET</p></div></section><section class="_step_ty83r_1"><h2 class="_stepTitle_ty83r_26">Step 4: The Final Step:Untethering Your Divice</h2><div class="_subtitle_20_6jn54_93 _artifakt_element_6jn54_2 _stepBody_1dglo_1"><p>please go to a tutorial on cydia. I recommend <a href="http://www.ipadforums.net/jailbreaking-hacking-tutorials-how-guides/67553-tutorial-cydia-beginners-guide-jailbreak-app-store.html" rel="nofollow noopener noreferrer">http://www.ipadforums.net/jailbreaking-hacking-tut...</a> . </p><p>Once you know the ropes, go to <strong>search.</strong> search <strong>p0sixspwn. </strong>click install at the top right(once it is installed, that tab will be switched to modify, like mine). your iPod will restart and when it comes back on, your done.</p></div></section><section class="_step_ty83r_1"><h2 class="_stepTitle_ty83r_26">Step 5: Some Recommendations</h2><div class="_subtitle_20_6jn54_93 _artifakt_element_6jn54_2 _stepBody_1dglo_1"><p>Now that your iPod is jailbroken, here are some Cyida apps.</p><p>look up how to install them online</p><p>1-winterboard - to customize your iPod(note, you will need to download mods through winter board)</p><p>2-AppCake- to get paid apps for free(NOTE: NOT LEAGAL)</p><p>3-activator - for instant and easy flow on your iPod</p><p>Have fun</p><p>-Shadow513</p></div></section></div></article><div class="_promoSide_19m79_7 _vertical_19m79_61" data-testid="promo-banner"><div class="_promoStickyContainer_19m79_13"><button class="_promoHide_19m79_18" aria-label="Close ad"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="#707070" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="24" height="24" aria-hidden="true"><path fill="#000" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4.3 4.3a1 1 0 0 1 1.4 0l6.3 6.29 6.3-6.3a1 1 0 1 1 1.4 1.42L13.42 12l6.3 6.3a1 1 0 0 1-1.42 1.4L12 13.42l-6.3 6.3a1 1 0 0 1-1.4-1.42L10.58 12l-6.3-6.3a1 1 0 0 1 0-1.4" clip-rule="evenodd"></path></svg></button><a href="https://www.instructables.com/contest/yoursforthemaking1?utm_source=instructables_us&amp;utm_medium=int_ref&amp;utm_campaign=edumkt_la28" class="_promoLink_19m79_30" aria-label="Advertisement"><picture><source type="image/webp" srcset="//images.ctfassets.net/jl5ii4oqrdmc/6x0GyLUlNQitIQQpdgRIsz/5e8ea71d251b205682cf739a41bb5bec/26_020_FM_Student_contest_Ad_600x1200_2x-100.jpg?w=300&amp;fm=webp 1x, //images.ctfassets.net/jl5ii4oqrdmc/6x0GyLUlNQitIQQpdgRIsz/5e8ea71d251b205682cf739a41bb5bec/26_020_FM_Student_contest_Ad_600x1200_2x-100.jpg?w=600&amp;fm=webp 2x"><img width="300" height="600" alt="Promo" src="//images.ctfassets.net/jl5ii4oqrdmc/6x0GyLUlNQitIQQpdgRIsz/5e8ea71d251b205682cf739a41bb5bec/26_020_FM_Student_contest_Ad_600x1200_2x-100.jpg?w=300&amp;fm=webp"></picture></a></div></div></div><div class="_footerWrapper_1kd8x_1"><div class="_contestEntriesWrapper_1e3g2_1"></div><section class="_IMadeIts_n8ptv_1" id="imadeits"><h2>Be the First to Share</h2><div class="_IMadeItGrid_n8ptv_13" data-testid="imadeit-grid"></div><div class="_IMadeItCTA_n8ptv_59 _subtitle_16_6jn54_105 _artifakt_element_6jn54_2"><p>Did you make this project?
